# King Midas
Aliases: Midas
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[King Midas]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 190-716
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor III]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Special Behavior: Summons a wave of gold that can attack players.
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 165,000-4,300,000; [[Damage]]: 1,692-18,720
[Drops]
Drops: 4–7x [[Gold Essence]] 0–1x [[Midas Jewel]]
XP: 1,425-5,370 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 570-2148
[Metadata]
ID: KING_MIDAS_#; MASTER_KING_MIDAS_#
King Midas is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] found in Floor III-VII of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. He can drop the [[Midas Jewel]] and [[Gold Essence]].
## Behavior
King Midas wears a full set of [[Golden Armor]] at the beginning of his fight, providing a high amount of [[Defense]]. King Midas loses his boots at 10% of his [[Health]] missing, his leggings at 30% of his [[Health]] missing, and his chestplate at 60% of his [[Health]] missing. Every lost piece of armor lowers his [[Defense]] significantly, making King Midas much easier to fight as the battle progresses.

## Trivia
- Under normal circumstances, in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]], King Midas has 4M [[Health]] health. However, if you clear every room except the Midas room, and use a Conjuring / Guided Sheep to blow up the crypt without walking into the room, then the next time you walk in following that, King Midas will have an upwards of 11M [[Health]] heath.
  - Interestingly enough, the same applies to all floors where King Midas can spawn.
- The description of [[Midas Jewel]] and the name of its ability imply that King Midas has a brother named Byron.
- Three months after the release of King Midas, an item with same ability as the one used by him was released, [[Midas Staff]].
- Prior to Update 0.19.4, spawning King Midas would mark his room as completed, and killing him had no effect on the party's [[Dungeon Score]].

# L.A.S.R.
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[L.A.S.R.]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 543
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 25,000,000; [[Damage]]: 17,600
[Drops]
XP: +4,072 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1629
[Metadata]
ID: SADAN_GIANT_1
L.A.S.R. is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that appears in the [[Sadan]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]. In [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], it is replaced with the [[Mutant Giant]].
## Spawning
L.A.S.R. appears in the [[Sadan]] fight arena in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]. It drops down to attack the player after [[Sadan|Sadan's Interest Level]] has been emptied for the second phase of the fight. In [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], all four giants are replaced with [[Mutant Giant|Mutant Giants]].
## Behavior
L.A.S.R. will run towards the player, damaging them when they get within range. They also shoot a laser at the player that deals 0.5x the base [[Damage]], or 8,800 . This fires every half second, for about 5 seconds, after which the player will receive a message for how much damage was dealt, and it will go on cooldown for 5 seconds.

- L.A.S.R. has the same internal ID of SADAN_GIANT_1 as the other giants, including [[The Diamond Giant]], the [[Jolly Pink Giant]], and [[Bigfoot]].

## Spawning
L.A.S.R. has a 25% (1 in 4 spawns) chance to be spawned from [[The Watcher]] in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
## Behavior
L.A.S.R. will run towards the player, damaging them when they get within range. They also shoot a laser at the player that deals 0.5x the base [[Damage]], or 5,760 in regular mode and 60,750 in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].

# Laser Guardian
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Confirmations needed, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Laser Guardian]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 197-508
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor III]]
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000-120,000,000; [[Damage]]: 4,320-81,000 (Base)
[Drops]
XP: 295-762 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 216-558
[Metadata]
ID: PROFESSOR_ARCHER_GUARDIAN_1; MASTER_PROFESSOR_ARCHER_GUARDIAN_1
The Laser Guardian is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] during [[The Professor]]'s fight.
## Spawning
The Laser Guardian spawns in one of the four containers of [[The Professor]]'s fighting arena. When killed, it will respawn 10 seconds later, unless the other three guardians ( [[Healthy Guardian]], [[Chaos Guardian]], and [[Reinforced Guardian]]) have also been killed.
Each respawn reduces the max [[Health]] by 20% in normal mode and 10% in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], stacking multiplicatively between respawns. Additionally, in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], the respawn timer is reduced to 7 seconds.
## Behavior
### Laser
By default, the Laser Guardian will fire a standard, slower laser at the player, attacking every 0.75 seconds using its base [[Damage]].
### Focus Beam
Starting 10s into the fight, the Laser Guardian will begin the following looping process:
- Announce that the Focus Beam is being charged up. This disables the default laser, and takes 15s in normal mode or 10 seconds in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
- Start firing the higher powered Focus Beam instead of the default laser. This attacks at a faster 0.5s rate and has a higher reach, but only deals 0.1x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (432 ), or 0.6x it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] (48,600 ). This laser lasts for 60s in normal mode and 45s in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
- Announce that the Focus Beam has overheated and is on cooldown, which lasts for 10s.
- Repeat this process.
If the Laser Guardian dies, once it respawns, the same cooldown message is sent 5s after, meaning the laser starts building up 15s later.
### Body Slam
When the player gets too close for long enough, the Laser Guardian will slam itself onto the ground, dealing immense [[Knockback]], 2x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (8,640 ), or 6x it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] (486,000 ).

XP: 412-817 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 302-599
[Metadata]
ID: WATCHER_LIVID_#; MASTER_WATCHER_LIVID_#
Livid is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that can be summoned by [[The Watcher]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] and above. They also [[Livid|appear]] as a [[Bosses|BOSS]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor V]].
## Spawning
Livid can be spawned from [[The Watcher]] in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] and above.
## Behavior
Livid will run towards the player to attack them. They can also blind the player, use the same dagger abilities from [[Livid|boss fight]], and teleport behind the player.

# Lonely Spider
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons, Dungeon Mobs
[Lonely Spider]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 31-315
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 10%
Spawn Condition: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Mob Type: [[Arthropod]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 3,500-2,000,000; [[Damage]]: 176-70,200
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Spider Essence]]
XP: 46-472 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 34-346
[Metadata]
ID: LONELY_SPIDER_#; MASTER_LONELY_SPIDER_#
The Lonely Spider is a [[Spider]] that acts normally. Lonely Spiders can be found in the [[Dungeons]], in a space full of other Lonely Spiders. Using an attack with a large AOE (area of effect) helps with milestones due to the amount of spiders in a room.
Its [[Health]], like any other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]], scales depending on which floor they spawn in. It can also have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].
## Behavior
- The Lonely Spider will jump toward the player, inflicting damage.

[Metadata]
ID: LOST_ADVENTURER_#
The Lost Adventurer is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Spawning
Lost Adventurers spawn in any floor of the [[Catacombs]]. They can either appear alone in yellow rooms (as shown on the [[Magical Map|map]]) or in normal rooms.
## Behavior
The Lost Adventurer holds an [[Aspect of the Dragons]] and occasionally uses the Dragon Rage ability. When players are out of their reach, the adventurer can switch to use bows to perform ranged attacks.
This mob acts like a player and can eat [[Golden Apple|Golden Apples]] to regenerate. However, each time the mob eats a Golden Apple, the health regeneration that it gives will decrease. Like other dungeon mobs, they naturally regenerate if they have not been attacked in a while.
If they are spawned in yellow rooms and either have not been attacked in a while or are not engaging to attack any players, they will teleport back to the center of their yellow room.
It also gains unique abilities based on the Dragon Armor it is wearing, which are similar to the [[Dragon Armor|Dragon Armor's]] set bonuses:
- If it's wearing [[Holy Dragon Armor]], it regenerates three times as fast.
- If it's wearing [[Unstable Dragon Armor]], it sometimes strikes nearby players with lightning, which deals 10% of the player's max [[Health]] in [[True Damage]].
- If it's wearing [[Young Dragon Armor]], it gains bonus movement speed when it's above 50% health.
- If it's wearing [[Superior Dragon Armor]], its Dragon Breath ability deals 50% more damage.
## Variants
Lost Adventurers can spawn in different sets of [[Dragon Armor]], specifically the following:
- [[Young Dragon Armor]]
- [[Unstable Dragon Armor]]
- [[Holy Dragon Armor]]
- [[Superior Dragon Armor]] (Rare)

## Gallery
- An armor-less Superior and Young Lost Adventurer
- An armor-less Unstable Lost Adventurer
- An armor-less Holy Lost Adventurer
## Trivia
- Even though the [[Aspect of the Dragons]] ability is called Dragon Rage, when a Lost Adventurer uses it, it's referred to as Dragon's Breath.
- The Lost Adventurers are immune to the Dragon's Rage ability even though they can use their equivalent Dragon's Breath.
- If an Archer's Bone Plating ability successfully blocks a Dragon's Breath, they would not be knocked back.
- They are rarer on floors 3 and above.
- For some reason, there are no Lost Adventurers wearing Old, Protector, Strong, or Wise dragon armor.
- The Dragon's Breath also deals knockback to other monsters.
## Tips
- It is recommended to not fight these mobs if you don't have the right armor and weapon as they can deal massive damage to players who are not ready.

# Mimic
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Mimic]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 231-568
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 50% (Not in M6 ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]))

[Metadata]
ID: MIMIC_115; MIMIC_125; MASTER_MIMIC_115; MASTER_MIMIC_125
The Mimic is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that sometimes spawns from [[Dungeon Secrets|Dungeon Secret]] chests.
## Spawning
Mimics rarely take the place of secret chests in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] dungeon. They appear once on every dungeon that is [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] or higher. The chests Mimics are found in are always a Trapped Chest.

## Trivia
- When Mimics were first added at the release of [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]], they could accidentally spawn in any [[Catacombs]] floor, even the [[The Catacombs - Entrance]]. They also have the same [[Health]] as the ones in Floor VI. It was then later patched and can only spawn in Floor VI and higher.

# Mutant Giant
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Confirmations needed, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Mutant Giant]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 1,129
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 600,000,000; [[Damage]]: 1,000,000
[Drops]
XP: +8,467 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 3387
[Metadata]
ID: MASTER_SADAN_GIANT_1
The Mutant Giant is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that appears four times in the [[Sadan]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|The Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VI]]. It replaces [[Bigfoot]], [[L.A.S.R.]], [[The Diamond Giant]], and the [[Jolly Pink Giant]].
## Spawning
The Mutant Giant appears four times in the [[Sadan]] fight arena in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]]. They drop down to attack the player after [[Sadan|Sadan's Interest Level]] has been emptied for the second phase of the fight. Once all four are killed and [[Sadan]] becomes The Giant One, four Mutant Giants spawn again.
## Behavior
The Mutant Giants will run towards the player, damaging them when they get within range. They have the two randomly selected abilities of [[Sadan]]'s giants, including [[Bigfoot]], [[L.A.S.R.]], [[The Diamond Giant]], and the [[Jolly Pink Giant]]:
|Original Giant|Possible Ability|
|-|-|
|[[Bigfoot]]|Stomps onto the ground every 7 seconds, dealing up to 4x the base [[Damage]], or 4,000,000 . The further away the player is, the less [[Damage]] they take.|
|[[L.A.S.R.]]|Shoots a laser at the player that deals 0.5x the base [[Damage]], or 500,000 . This fires every half second, for about 5 seconds, after which it goes on cooldown for 5 seconds.|
|[[The Diamond Giant]]|Occasionally throws a giant sword down onto the player, dealing 200,000 [[Damage]].|
|[[Jolly Pink Giant]]|Occasionally throws a boulder towards the player, dealing 3x the base [[Damage]], or 3,000,000 . The further away the player is, the less [[Damage]] they take.|
## Drops
Non-item drops - [Lv1,129]:
|Drop|Count|
|-|-|
|[[Coins]]|2,822|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|8,467|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|3,387|

|-|-|
|Mutant Giant hit you with Laser Eyes for # damage!|Being hit by the laser eyes (message sent once it goes on cooldown)|
|Mutant Giant hit you with Stomp for # damage!|Being hit by the stomp|
|Mutant Giant hit you with Sword of 50,000 Truths for # damage!|Being hit by the sword|

## Spawning

## Behavior

# Prince
Aliases: Prince Alexander, Prince Bernhard, Prince Christian, Prince Friedrich, Prince Marius, Prince Nicholas, Prince Pieter, Prince Valentin
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Huntable Creatures, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs

ID: CRYPT_UNDEAD_<NAME>_40; MASTER_CRYPT_UNDEAD_<NAME>_40
Princes are [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]] found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] and a tougher version of [[Crypt Undead|Crypt Undeads]].
## Spawning
Princes can only be spawned after blowing up certain Crypt tomb in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] with [[Superboom TNT]], the [[Mage]] Guided Sheep ability, or the [[Conjuring]]'s ability. These crypts are often tied to [[Dungeon Secrets]]. A random Prince is chosen, with the names including Valentin, Nicholas, Pieter, Alexander, Christian, Marius, Bernhard, and Friedrich.
## Behavior
Like [[Crypt Undead|Crypt Undeads]], Princes attack by running towards the player. Although they wield a [[Bone]], they won't attack the player with it. It can also attack the player with a bow if you are far from it.

## Behavior
The Redstone Warrior holds a [[Stone Sword]] that occasionally uses the [[Aspect of the Dragons]]'s Dragon Rage ability. When players are out of their reach, the warrior can switch to use bows to perform ranged attacks.
The warrior can eat [[Golden Apple|Golden Apples]] to regenerate. However, each time one is consumed, the [[Health]] regeneration that it gives will decrease. Like other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], it will naturally regenerate [[Health]] if they've not been attacked in a while.

|-|-|
|The Redstone Warrior used Dragon's Breath on you!|When Dragon Breath is used|

## Trivia
- The Redstone Warrior internally is a Young [[Lost Adventurer]], which means killing one grants [[Bestiary]] credit for one.
  - When it kills a player or hits them with the [[Aspect of the Dragons]]' Dragon's Breath Ability it will say "killed by [[Lost Adventurer]]" instead of "killed by Redstone Warrior".
- Its head has the same texture as the [[Redstone Key]].

# Reinforced Guardian
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Reinforced Guardian]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 207-516
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor III]]
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,200,000-140,000,000; [[Damage]]: 5,040-81,000 (Base)
[Drops]
XP: 310-774 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 227-567
[Metadata]
ID: PROFESSOR_WARRIOR_GUARDIAN_1; MASTER_PROFESSOR_WARRIOR_GUARDIAN_1
The Reinforced Guardian is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] during [[The Professor]]'s fight.
## Spawning
The Reinforced Guardian spawns in one of the four containers of [[The Professor]]'s fighting arena. When killed, it will respawn 10 seconds later, unless the other three guardians ( [[Healthy Guardian]], [[Chaos Guardian]], and [[Laser Guardian]]) have also been killed.
Each respawn reduces the max [[Health]] by 20% in normal mode and 10% in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], stacking multiplicatively between respawns. Additionally, in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], the respawn timer is reduced to 7 seconds.
## Behavior
### TNT Rain
Every ~40 seconds (or ~30s in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]]), the Reinforced Guardian will start throwing TNT around itself for 10 seconds. This TNT deals 0.6x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (3,024 ), or 0.8x it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] (64,800 ).
This timer runs independently of the Reinforged Guardian's alive state, meaning killing it does not impact this timer. If the TNT rain were to start when the boss is otherwise dead, it gets skipped.
### Rogue Guardians
The Reinforced Guardian can repeatedly spawn [[Rogue Guardian|Rogue Guardians]] around itself.
### Body Slam
When the player gets too close for long enough, the Reinforced Guardian will slam itself onto the ground, dealing immense [[Knockback]], 2x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (10,080 ), or 6x it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] (486,000 ).

## Trivia
The Reinforced Guardian was seemingly originally going to be called the Protector Guardian, due to its TNT Rain message using that name.

# Rogue Guardian

ID: PROFESSOR_GUARDIAN_SUMMON_1; PROFESSOR_GUARDIAN_SUMMON_2; MASTER_PROFESSOR_GUARDIAN_SUMMON_2
Rogue Guardians are a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]] that spawn in [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] during [[The Professor]]'s fight.
## Spawning
Rogue Guardians will repeatedly spawn throughout [[The Professor]]'s fighting arena in the air. There are two versions, one that is a Guardian, and a higher tier Elder Guardian version.
During the first phase of the fight, they spawn near the [[Reinforced Guardian]]. Later on, they spawn around [[The Professor]].
## Behavior
Rogue Guardians fly throughout the arena, firing lasers at nearby players. IAfter being alive for some time, they will turn into stronger elder guardians.

# Sadan
Aliases: LASR, Lasr, Sadans's Giants, The Giant One
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Pages with UIs, Catacombs, Dungeons, Bosses, Boss Collections
Necromancy was always strong in his family. Says he once beat a Wither in a duel. Likes to brag.
[Sadan]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 611-1,097
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]
Description: Necromancer Lord; [[Necromancy]] was always strong in his family. Says he once beat a Wither in a duel. Likes to brag.
Mob Type: [[Arcane]] [[Humanoid]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 40,000,000-800,000,000; [[Damage]]: 30,960-720,000
[Drops]
XP: 4,582-8,227 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1833-3291
[Metadata]
ID: SADAN_1; MASTER_SADAN_1
Sadan is the [[Bosses|BOSS]] of [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]], and as a character appearing in the [[Journal|dungeon journals]] [[Journal|Aftermath]] and [[Journal|The Eye]]. His status is the Necromancer Lord.
## Collection
Killing Sadan increases the Sadan collection.
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Giant Tooth]] Reward|+15|
|II|100|Golden Sadan Head ([[Golden Heads]]) Reward; Gold ([[Gold Essence]]) Essence|+15|
|III|150|Necromancer Lord Helmet ([[Necromancer Lord Armor]]) Reward|+15|
|IV|250|[[Recombobulator 3000]] Reward|+25|
|V|500|Necromancer Lord Chestplate ([[Necromancer Lord Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|VI|750|[[Necromancer Sword]] Reward|+25|
|VII|1,000|Diamond Sadan Head ([[Diamond Heads]]) Recipe; Diamond ([[Diamond Essence]]) Essence|+25|
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Boss Fight
Sadan's boss fight takes place in a large, tall hall with two wings, one of which the players emerge from. When the players enter, 16 dormant [[Terracotta|Terracottas]] stand along the hall. [[Golem|Sleeping Golems]] reside in the main chamber of Sadan's room, and attacking them will turn them into [[Golem|Woke Golem]]. Above are four cages, each with a Giant inside it, and a large section beneath containing a larger Giant.
### Phase 1
During Sadan's first phase, he reanimates several [[Terracotta|Terracottas]] that will start to damage the player, dealing melee and ranged damage. These will attack the players for 2 minutes, displayed by a timer labeled Sadan's Interest Meter: killing a [[Terracotta]] will reduce this timer by 1 second.
Once killed, [[Terracotta|Terracottas]] will turn into a skull on the ground. After 10 seconds, they will respawn where they died and attack the player until Sadan's Interest Meter is depleted.
### Phase 2
Four different giants, being [[The Diamond Giant]], [[L.A.S.R.]], [[Bigfoot]], and the [[Jolly Pink Giant]], will drop from cages in the sky to attack. Each giant has a different attack and has a chance to appear as [[The Watcher|Watcher Undeads]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
- [[The Diamond Giant]] will damage players with the Sword of 1,000 Truths.
- [[L.A.S.R.]] will fire at players with a the L.A.S.R Eye Beam, dealing increased damage over time.
- [[Bigfoot]] will use Stomp on players, dealing high amounts of damage.
- The [[Jolly Pink Giant]] will use Boulder Toss on players, throwing a large boulder at players and dealing large damage.
### Phase 3
A cutscene will play where Sadan will merge with his "Ultimate Corpse", The Giant One, and become a Giant himself. A hole will open in the center of the hall, and a hook will appear to lift the Giant out. Falling into the hole instantly kills the player who fell in and all [[Tank|Tanks]] in the team (because of [[Tank|Tanks]]' passive ability Diversion). After this cutscene, the hole closes, allowing players to safely walk over it again.
In the form of The Giant One, Sadan has all the perks of the previous Giants combined. Players win the fight by defeating Sadan in this Giant form. Unlike the previous giants, The Giant One does not have [[Knockback]] immunity.

- Instead of the normal giants, [[Mutant Giant|Mutant Giants]] will spawn with 600,000,000 [[Health]], and can use all two randomly selected abilities of the other giants at once.
- After the cutscene, the 4 [[Mutant Giant|Mutant Giants]] respawn at the 4 pillars together with Sadan to fight the players.

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Boss Fight Start|[BOSS] Sadan: So you made it all the way here... Now you wish to defy me? Sadan?! [BOSS] Sadan: The audacity! I have been the ruler of these floors for a hundred years! [BOSS] Sadan: I am the bridge between this realm and the world below! You shall not pass!|
|1 Giant Killed|[BOSS] Sadan: You've managed to kill a Giant! Good luck with the rest of them! [BOSS] Sadan: Wow, one Giant down already? You guys are good! [BOSS] Sadan: How many players does it take to kill a Giant? [BOSS] Sadan: You killed my weakest Giant!|
|2 Giants Killed|[BOSS] Sadan: Uh oh! Half of my Giant creations have bit the dust! Whatever will I do? [BOSS] Sadan: 2 of my Giants down?! No matter, these last two are tough! [BOSS] Sadan: That Giant was weak! I clearly screwed up during the necromancy process.|
|3 Giants Killed|[BOSS] Sadan: I'm starting to sweat! [BOSS] Sadan: Down to 1 Giant! [BOSS] Sadan: Whoops! There goes my third Giant!|
|First Golem Woken|[BOSS] Sadan: Interesting strategy, waking up my Golems. Or was that unintentional, [player]? [BOSS] Sadan: You weren't supposed to wake up that Golem, [player]! [BOSS] Sadan: My Terracotta Army wasn't enough? You had to awaken a Golem on top, [player]?! [BOSS] Sadan: Those Golems will be your undoing, [player]!|
|Golem Woken|[BOSS] Sadan: How many more of my Golems will you disturb, [player]? [BOSS] Sadan: Reckless humans! You should know by now my Golems do not want to be awoken, [player]! [BOSS] Sadan: All my Golems want is some peace and quiet! Can you not, [player]?!|
|Golem Woken; by a Player Summon ([[Necromancy]])|[BOSS] Sadan: How did that Golem wake up? Carelessness from you adventurers, no doubt.|
|All Golems Woken|[BOSS] Sadan: Thanks to [player], all of my Golems have been awoken! Quite the feat! [BOSS] Sadan: Wonderous! Not one sleeping Golem was left unperturbed by you reckless humans! Thanks, [player]! [BOSS] Sadan: I've never seen such carelessness in my years as a Necromancer! So little regard for my Golems, and even less regard for your own lives! Blame [player]!|
|Phase One|[BOSS] Sadan: My Terracotta Army is using some of the finest souls - old warriors and wizards! [BOSS] Sadan: You will be humbled, reckless humans! [BOSS] Sadan: Can you at least enjoy my science, pushing the boundaries of Necromancy! [BOSS] Sadan: Corpses are deficient, full of weaknesses. Statues are stronger, faster, and easier to repair. [BOSS] Sadan: Can't you see that there is no winning? This room will be your tomb. [BOSS] Sadan: So much effort, for what? You should have joined us instead, Necron will cure this world! [BOSS] Sadan: Terrible...terrible..., [BOSS] Sadan: I'm growing tired of this folly. My Giants are restless. [BOSS] Sadan: It's about time we see what my Giants can do...|
|Phase Two|[BOSS] Sadan: Rumor has it those are dead Precursors, legendary civilization. [BOSS] Sadan: My first time using them, extremely interesting! [BOSS] Sadan: Which one is your favorite? Those laser eyes...beautiful! [BOSS] Sadan: This is what peak Necromancy looks like! [BOSS] Sadan: Boulders, lasers, stomping, and giant swords! Oh my!|
|Phase Two Starts|[BOSS] Sadan: ENOUGH! [BOSS] Sadan: My giants! Unleashed!|
|Phase Three Starts|[BOSS] Sadan: You did it. I understand now, you have earned my respect. [BOSS] Sadan: If only you had become my disciples instead of this incompetent bunch. [BOSS] Sadan: Maybe in another life. Until then, meet my ultimate corpse. [BOSS] Sadan: I'm sorry but I need to concentrate. I wish it didn't have to come to this.|
|Phase Three Master Mode Giants Revive|[BOSS] Sadan: You thought I'd fall for the same tricks as those before me? Not today!|
|Boss Wins|[BOSS] Sadan: It was inevitable. You are fighting forces beyond your imagination. [BOSS] Sadan: No adventurer can see what lays beyond this floor. [BOSS] Sadan: Rest in peace.|
|Final Kill|[BOSS] Sadan: NOOOOOOOOO!!! THIS IS IMPOSSIBLE!! [BOSS] Sadan: FATHER, FORGIVE ME!!!|
## Lore
- In [[Time Systems|1531 AE]], after zombies began leaving [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] a human king sent an expedition with 3 mages to investigate. Mages were the Royal Inscriptionist, who kept a four-volume journal about the expedition until his death, [[Solomon]] and Sadan.
- During the expedition, Sadan noticed [[The Watcher|an Eye]] looking directly at him, but no one else seemed to notice it, so he kept it to himself, since it didn't seem hostile. It appeared 3 times throughout the first and second floor, then disappeared. However, on the third floor, he felt a different sensation: he sensed something coming even before more talented [[Solomon]]. Sadan managed to escape the massacre caused by [[Necron|the Wither]] that appeared and killed [[General Dan]], then he felt the familiar sensation of being watched. He pulled out his sword, asking "What do you want from me?", [[The Watcher]] came out of the shadows and questioned him
- Some time after his interaction with the Watcher, he became Necromancer Lord and guarded the Sixth floor of Catacombs. He learned that his parents were powerful in Necromancy, which is why Watcher was interested in him.
- In 1532 AE, he learned that his father was still alive, if you could call it that. He was a member of [[Necron]]'s Wither Army - [[Storm]]. But his father didn't care for him, which made Sadan angry, and he swore that he would develop a technique to surpass even a Wither with his own abilities.
- According to [[Mort]]'s description of Sadan, he succeeded, but it is unclear if the Wither in question was [[Storm]], his unknown professor or a different Wither entirely.
## Trivia
- The boss appears to have an egotistical and self-centered nature, possibly caused by his father, [[Storm]] not caring about him and him choosing power rather than humanity. He shares his ego with his father, and they both seem to make up with each other when faced with death, as their last words reference each other.
- This boss used to be a part of the Expedition team as one of at least 3 mages. Because his family was skilled in Necromancy, [[The Watcher]] saw his potential to be a servant of Necron. In the journal Aftermath, it is said that Solomon (One of the mages on the Expedition team) was also pulled aside by Necron and executed by Storm for him to gain immense power his third wither head.
- The sword that the Diamond Giant wields is actually called Diamante (which translates to "Diamond" in Spanish and Italian).
- His head has the same texture as the [[The Professor|Professor's]], except recoloured.
- The Terracotta mob is a reference to the terracotta army in the Emperor Qinshihuang's Mausoleum in China.
- The name Sadan could be a reference to the devil, Satan and his number 666. This could also explain why Sadan is the floor 6 boss in the Catacombs.

The Scared Skeleton is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] and below.
## Spawning
Scared Skeletons spawn in rooms within [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] and lower floors.
## Behavior
When a player is near an Scared Skeleton, it will try to run away from the player, instead of attacking them.

Scarf is the [[Bosses|BOSS]] of [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] within [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]].
## Collection
Killing Scarf increases the Scarf dungeon collection.

Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Boss Fight
The Scarf fight has two distinct phases.
In the first phase, Scarf is protected by a barrier (reversed world border), preventing players from attacking him. Players must defeat Scarf's Undeads, which include an [[Undead Priest]], [[Undead Mage]], [[Undead Warrior]], and [[Undead Archer]]. Each of the Undead works differently and use attacks or abilities corresponding to their role.
In the second phase, the barrier protecting Scarf disappears, and begins attacking players directly. All the Undead are also revived and become stronger. Scarf acts similar to the Warrior but uses many more abilities and is stronger.
- It is recommended to kill the [[Undead Priest]] first in this phase, as he will heal Scarf if you do not kill him fast enough.
### Attacks
Scarf and each mob will run towards the player to attack them. They also have additional, special attacks, as listed below:
|Mob|Attacks|
|-|-|
|Scarf|Skull Barrage - Scarf rises into the air, hovering slightly above the ground and barraging all players with explosive Wither Skeleton skulls. This move fires in a wide arc, causing many of the shots to miss or strike other players. Ghosts can tank the skulls from this ability. Soul Sand Blizzard - Scarf summons soul sand blocks in a path towards the player, dealing a percentage[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) of the player's max [[Health]] as [[True Damage]] to players who step in it. This also gives slowness and decreased FOV.|
|[[Undead Warrior]]|Charge - Occasionally charges at the player, dealing 0.3x the base [[Damage]] (864 ) in regular mode, or 1x the base [[Damage]] (54,000 ) in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]).|
|[[Undead Archer]]|Arrow Barrage - Shoots a large number of arrows in all directions. Teleport - Teleport himself away from danger, or a player into danger (Usually Warrior's charge). Help - Calls for help, causing the Warrior to perform a Warrior's Charge to the player who dealt the most damage to the archer,|
|[[Undead Mage]]|Wither Skulls - Shoots wither skulls towards the player while standing still, dealing 0.45x the base [[Damage]] (1,094.4 ) in regular mode and 0.65x (29,640 ) it in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]). Magma Pit - Deals 0.15x the base [[Damage]] (364.8 ) in regular mode and 0.6x (27,360 ) it in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]). Tsunami - Creates a wave that deals 0.25x the base [[Damage]] (608 ) in regular mode and 0.5x the base [[Damage]] (22,800 ) in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]). Help - Calls for help, causing the [[Undead Warrior]] to perform a Warrior's Charge to the player who dealt the most damage to the mage.|
|[[Undead Priest]]|Heal - Heals up itself and nearby Undeads. Help - Calls for help, causing the [[Undead Warrior]] to perform a Warrior's Charge to the player who dealt the most damage to the priest.|

### Master Mode Changes
- Scarf has 375M [[Health]] and his Undeads have significantly more [[Health]] . His attacks deal much more damage.
- In both phases, 2 of each type of Undead spawn instead of 1.
- In the 2nd phase, Scarf gains damage resistance based on how many Undeads are alive. He will be completely immune to damage until at least 4 undeads are defeated.

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Intro|[BOSS] Scarf: This is where the journey ends for you, Adventurers. [BOSS] Scarf: The last few who tried fighting me are now in those Crypts. [BOSS] Scarf: If you can beat my Undeads, I'll personally grant you the privilege to replace them. [BOSS] Scarf: ARISE, MY CREATIONS! [BOSS] Scarf: This should be interesting.|
|Start of Phase 2|[BOSS] Scarf: Those toys are not strong enough I see. [BOSS] Scarf: Don't get too excited though. [BOSS] Scarf: Did you forget? I was taught by the best! Let's dance.|
|During Phase 2|[BOSS] Scarf: Where is my Priest! [BOSS] Scarf: Warrior! Charge this fool! [BOSS] Scarf: Back off! [BOSS] Scarf: OUCH! [BOSS] Scarf: Oof [BOSS] Scarf: You'd make a great Undead minion! [BOSS] Scarf: I can't believe Bonzo lost to someone so weak!|
|Upon minion kill|[BOSS] Scarf: I'll make you take the (minion)'s place! [BOSS] Scarf: Oh, you killed my (minion)? No problem. [BOSS] Scarf: Darn! My (minion)! [BOSS] Scarf: RIP (minion). F [BOSS] Scarf: I expected better from you, (minion).|
|During Skull Barrage|[BOSS] Scarf: No more hiding! [BOSS] Scarf: I'll send you to the Wither Realm! [BOSS] Scarf: Just who do you think I am! [BOSS] Scarf: Dodge this! [BOSS] Scarf: Soon I'll be shooting your skull! [BOSS] Scarf: Necro no Jutsu! [BOSS] Scarf: Yare yare daze... [BOSS] Scarf: I'm going to be the Wither King! [BOSS] Scarf: Wither Ultra!|
|All minions dead|[BOSS] Scarf: Fine then! I'll take you myself!|
|After dealing major damage to [[Scarf]] Scarf|[BOSS] Scarf: That was nothing my Priest can't heal!|
|If the players have lost|[BOSS] Scarf: I always knew I would win...I'm a genius. [BOSS] Scarf: But don't worry I'll add you to my collection. [BOSS] Scarf: In a way, you'll live forever...|
|If the players have won|[BOSS] Scarf: Whatever... [BOSS] Scarf: You'll never beat my teacher.. [BOSS] Scarf: His technique.. is too advanced..|
## Trivia
- A reason Scarf was the top student in his class was that he hoped he could bring his mother back. This is suggested by the [[Red Scarf]] reforge stone, as his mother knit him that Scarf.
- One of Scarf's dialogue lines is "Yare yare daze"; this is a reference to JoJo's Bizarre Adventure.
- Another of Scarf's dialogue lines is "Necro no Jutsu!"; this is a reference to Naruto.
- Another is "If you win, you live. If you lose, you die. If you don't fight, you can't win." which is most likely a reference to Attack on Titan.
- The [[Adaptive Blade]], a drop from Scarf, is a reference that Scarf and all his Undead represent all the classes players can choose from, with Scarf being the [[Tank]].
- Scarf is revived by [[The Watcher]] on [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Floor 4]] , [[The Catacombs - Floor V|5]] , [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|6]] only having Scarf in his Second phase.
- When Scarf summoned as Undead is defeated, it doesn't add to the collection.
- The Undead Warrior(s) is/are currently bugged to charge up [[True Damage]] instead of regular Damage.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

# Scarf (Watcher)
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Scarf (Watcher)]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 201-540
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]] [[Arcane]]

[Drops]

XP: 301-810 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 221-594
[Metadata]
ID: WATCHER_SCARF_#; MASTER_WATCHER_SCARF_#
Scarf is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that can be summoned by [[The Watcher]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] and above. They also [[Scarf|appear]] as a [[Bosses|BOSS]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]].
## Spawning
Scarf can be spawned from [[The Watcher]] in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] and above.
## Behavior
Scarf will initially run towards the player to attack them with melee. Upon taking enough [[Damage]], they will start using their Soul Sand Blizzard attack. This creates soul sand blocks in a path towards the player, dealing 0.2x the base [[Damage]] while standing on them.

# Shadow Assassin
Aliases: Assassin
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons, Minibosses
I could sense a dark mass behind him, a man. "ALBERT!" I screamed as I lunged forward. My blade pierced through the assassin, and as I got closer, a light revealed my enemy.
— Grim Adversity (Page 4)
[Shadow Assassin]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 196-855
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor III]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Special Behavior: Can teleport to attack a certain teammate
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 385,000-145,000,000; [[Damage]]: 862-210,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Silent Death]] 0–1x [[Beating Heart]]
XP: 1,470-6,412 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 588-2565
[Metadata]
ID: SHADOW_ASSASSIN_#; MASTER_SHADOW_ASSASSIN_#
The Shadow Assassin is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] that appears from [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] onward.
## Spawning
Shadow Assassins spawn as Mini-Bosses in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] on Floor III and above. Their health and damage scales by their floor and dungeon progress; if spawned in a yellow room, it is significantly buffed.
### [[Storm]] Boss Fight
A total of 4 [Lv171] Shadow Assassins spawn in each corner of the arena during the 2nd phase of [[Storm]]'s fight.
## Behavior
The Shadow Assassin can teleport and is extremely agile. It has the highest melee damage of any mini-boss and does additional damage when attacking from behind. However, it does not have a healing ability. It is invisible until a player is nearby, upon which it will teleport to the player and become visible. It runs towards the player, occasionally teleporting behind them, and tries to hit them, dealing [[Damage]].
### [[Storm]] fight
The Shadow Assassins protect the four control pads for the crushers at the four corners of the room. [[Storm]] will try to protect them, attacking any player who attacks them.

## Trivia
- Shadow Assassins could have been [[Livid]]'s shadows that Livid's dialogue mentioned upon his defeat.
  - This is further suggested since they have an emblem on their body very similar to the emblem in [[Livid]]'s room.
- The [[Journal|Dungeon Journal]] Grim Adversity suggests that the narrator failed to protect someone of the name Albert from a Shadow Assassin.
- In Master Mode, Shadow Assassins can spawn an upwards of 370M health in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Master Mode - Floor VII]].
- When summoned using the Necromancy mechanic, Shadow Assassins appear to be floating iron swords.
- The [[Beating Heart]] drop is bugged and cannot drop from a Shadow Assassin.
- If a player is standing in front of a wall which has a space behind the wall, Shadow Assassins will teleport through the wall. When the wall is next to the void, they will die.
- The [[Ice Spray Wand]], [[Gyrokinetic Wand]] and [[Fire Freeze Staff]] can lock Shadow Assassins in place, which can be used to dispatch of them very quickly. However, this does not stop them from teleporting.

ID: SKELETON_GRUNT_#; MASTER_SKELETON_GRUNT_#
The Skeleton Grunt is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] and below.
## Spawning
Skeleton Grunts spawn in rooms within [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] - [[The Catacombs - Floor I|Floor I]] and [[The Catacombs - Entrance|Entrance]].
## Behavior
Skeleton Grunts will try to shoot the player with a Bow, dealing [[Damage Calculation|Projectile Damage]]. They fire at a much higher rate compared to other [[Skeleton|Skeletons]].

# Skeleton Master
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, More info needed, Mobs with Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Skeleton Master]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 82-496
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 30%
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor II]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Mob Type: [[Skeletal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 31,200-42,000,000; [[Damage]]: 945 - 182,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Bone Dye]] 0–1x Skeleton Master Armor Piece ([[Skeleton Master Armor]]) 0–1x [[Enchanted Bone]] 0–1x [[Machine Gun Shortbow]]
XP: 123-744 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 90-545
[Metadata]
ID: SKELETON_MASTER_#; MASTER_SKELETON_MASTER_#
The Skeleton Master is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns on [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] and above. It can use its [[Bows|Bow]] to attack players, and can fire a Wither Skull, dealing high damage.
## Spawning
Skeleton Masters can spawn within rooms on [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] and higher floors.
## Behavior
Skeleton Masters shoot [[Arrows]] with a [[Bows|Bow]], dealing [[Damage Calculation|Projectile Damage]]. They can shoot a Wither Skull, dealing ? [[True Damage]][More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.).
### Respawning
When it dies, it turns into a Skeleton Skull. After 2 or 3 skulls are merged, an [[Undead Skeleton]] will spawn. On [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] and above, 3 skeleton skulls merging together will form a [[Skeletor]]. On [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] and above, it can rarely turn into a [[Skeletor Prime]]

# Skeleton Soldier
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Skeleton Soldier]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 61-449
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 30%
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor I]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Mob Type: [[Skeletal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 22,400-28,000,000; [[Damage]]: 324-108,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x Skeleton Soldier Armor Piece ([[Skeleton Soldier Armor]]) 0–1x [[Bone Dye]]
XP: 91-673 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 67-493
[Metadata]
ID: SKELETON_SOLDIER_#; MASTER_SKELETON_SOLDIER_#
Skeleton Soldier is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns on [[The Catacombs - Floor I]] and above. They can use their [[Bows|Bow]] to attack players.
Like most [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], it can have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].
## Spawning
Skeleton Soldiers can spawn within rooms on [[The Catacombs - Floor I]] and higher floors.
## Behavior
It shoots [[Arrows]] with a [[Bows|Bow]], dealing [[Damage Calculation|Projectile Damage]]. It can shoot Arrows rapidly like a [[Machine Gun Shortbow]].
### Respawning
When it dies, it turns into a Skeleton Skull. After 2 or 3 skulls are merged, an [[Undead Skeleton]] will spawn. On [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] and above, 3 skeleton skulls merging together will form a [[Skeletor]]. On [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] and above, it can rarely turn into a [[Skeletor Prime]].

# Skeletor
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs, Dungeon Mobs
[Skeletor]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 149-396
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 30%
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor III]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) (Only M3 ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]])) [[Skeletal]] [[Construct]] (Not M3 ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]]))
Stats: [[Health]]: 250,000-10,200,000; [[Damage]]: 2,848-93,600
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Bone Dye]] 0–1x Skeletor Armor Piece ([[Skeletor Armor]])
XP: 223-594 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 163-435
[Metadata]
ID: SKELETOR_#; MASTER_SKELETOR_#
The Skeletor is a strong Skeleton which spawns in the [[Catacombs]] from 3 dead [[Skeleton (disambiguation)|Skeletons]] dropping their skulls in a 3-block radius. It drops [[Skeletor Armor]].
## Spawning
The Skeletor spawns in the on [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] (3) and above. On Floor VI, they have a 50% chance to be replaced by [[Skeletor Prime]], and on Floor VII, the chance is 100%. It is spawned when 3 Skeleton-type mobs die and drop their skulls within a 3-block radius. When its spawning conditions are met, an animation will play showing the 3 skulls merging and the Skeletor will spawn.
## Behavior
The Skeletor is hostile to all players. It will target the nearest player and run towards them at high speeds. It first throws its bone at players, similar to that of a [[Crypt Lurker]], and then rushes in to melee at players holding a bone, periodically throwing bones at their target. Both the ranged and melee attacks deal heavy [[True Damage]].

## Trivia
- Killing a Skeletor while its bone is in mid-flight will neutralize the bone, causing the player to take no damage from it.
- The Skeletor is possibly named after Skeletor, the main antagonist of the Masters of the Universe series.
- The Skeletor and the [[Skeletor Prime]] may be inspired by the Skeletron and the Skeletron Prime bosses from Terraria.

# Skeletor Prime
Aliases: Prime Skeletor
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Confirmations needed, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Skeletor Prime]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 227-451
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 30%
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Special Behavior: Created by three defeated skeletons merging on Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]) or higher.
Mob Type: [[Skeletal]] [[Construct]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,515,151-20,606,060; [[Damage]]: 7,680-153,600
[Drops]

XP: 340-676 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 249-496
[Metadata]
ID: SKELETOR_PRIME_#; MASTER_SKELETOR_PRIME_#
Skeletor Prime is a [[Mob]] that spawns in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. It is a stronger variant of [[Skeletor]]. Skeletor Primes are summoned when three skeletons are defeated close together and merge. They also deal [[True Damage]], making them an especially deadly mob even to more experienced Dungeons players.
## Spawning
A Skeletor Prime is spawned when three Skeleton Skulls are near each other and merge on [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] or higher. Skeleton Skulls are spawned when Skeleton-type enemies are killed. In The Catacombs - Floor VI, there is a 50% chance for a Skeletor Prime to spawn when three Skeleton Skulls merge, instead of a [[Skeletor]]. In [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]], there is a 100% chance for a Skeletor Prime to spawn.
## Behavior
When a player is nearby, a Skeletor Prime will run towards them and attempt to hit them, dealing damage. They will occasionally throw a Bone towards the player, dealing damage. Both attacks deal [[True Damage]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].

- These may be a reference to Skeletron Prime from Terraria.
- Their design may be a reference to Sans from Undertale.

The Sniper is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Spawning
Snipers can spawn in rooms within any floor in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Behavior
Snipers stay still and shoot [[Arrows]] with a [[Bows|Bow]], dealing high [[Damage Calculation|Projectile Damage]].

# Spirit Bat
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Spirit Bat]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 108-291
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 50,000-1,250,000 (Base); [[Defense]]: 1,000; [[Damage]]: 2,380-59,500
[Drops]
XP: 162-436 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 118-320
[Metadata]
ID: SPIRIT_BAT_1; MASTER_SPIRIT_BAT_1
The Spirit Bat is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] during the [[Thorn]] fight.
## Spawning
Spirit Bats spawn in during the [[Thorn]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]. The amount of [[Health]] they have increase with the wave of the fight, based on the following formula:
Health=BaseHealth+(Wave×BonusHealth)
The bonus [[Health]] per wave is 25,000 in regular mode, or 500,000 in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
## Behavior
Spirit Bats will swarm players who are in the air. They do not respect player invincibility frames. This means if a player jumps in the air and is swarmed by bats, they will be mowed to death in seconds. Can be deterred by decoys as they have no vanilla attacking ability and cannot destroy it.

# Spirit Bear
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Spirit Bear]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 408-848

Mob Type: [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5,000,000-180,000,000; [[Damage]]: 8,704-154,700
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Spirit Bow]]
XP: 3,060-6,360 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1224-2544
[Metadata]
ID: SPIRIT_MINIBOSS_130; MASTER_SPIRIT_MINIBOSS_130
The Spirit Bear is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] during the [[Thorn]] fight.
## Spawning
The Spirit Bear is summoned during the [[Thorn]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]. It is summoned once 25 Spirit Animals (or 30 in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]]) have been killed. This is indicated by a counter in the form of a ring of Sea Lanterns and Coal Blocks near the audience, which shows how many Spirit Animals have been killed. When the ring fully lights up with Sea Lanterns, the Spirit Bear will spawn.
## Behavior
The Spirit Bear attacks players with a Golden Sword or a [[Spirit Bow]]. It will also heal overtime, but very slowly.
When it is killed, it drops a [[Spirit Bow]]. The player who picks it up only gets one chance to shoot [[Thorn]]. Each successful hit removes 1 [[Health]] , stuns [[Thorn]] for 5s, and then triggers by a 1s cooldown before Thorn can be damaged again.

The Spirit Bull is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] during the [[Thorn]] fight.
## Spawning
Spirit Bulls spawn in during the [[Thorn]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]. The amount of [[Health]] they have increase with the wave of the fight, based on the following formula:
Health=BaseHealth+(Wave×BonusHealth)
The bonus [[Health]] per wave is 100,000 in regular mode, or 2,000,000 in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
## Behavior
Spirit Bulls rush towards the player to attack them. They also have a charge attack, dealing 30% of the base [[Damage]] in regular mode (846.6 ) and 75% of the base [[Damage]] in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] (53,550 ).

# Spirit Chicken
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Spirit Chicken]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 107-294
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]

[Drops]

[Metadata]
ID: SPIRIT_CHICKEN_1; MASTER_SPIRIT_CHICKEN_1
The Spirit Chicken is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] during the [[Thorn]] fight.
## Spawning
Spirit Chickens spawn in large groups from the ceiling during the [[Thorn]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]. The amount of [[Health]] they have increase with the wave of the fight, based on the following formula:
Health=BaseHealth+(Wave×BonusHealth)
The bonus [[Health]] per wave is 25,000 in regular mode, or 500,000 in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
## Behavior
Spirit Chickens slowly descends to the ground, strike nearby players with lightning. If it touches the ground, it will turn into a Chicken Mine. Players who walk over it take damage and are knocked into the air.
The lightning attacks deal 1.5x the base [[Damage]] (1,392 ) in regular mode, and 0.35x the base [[Damage]] (9,408 ) in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].

# Super Tank Zombie
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Super Tank Zombie]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 67-277
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 10% (0% in M4 ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]))
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 20,000-1,050,000; [[Defense]]: 2,500; [[Damage]]: 2,320-48,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Premium Flesh]] 0–1x [[Earthen Blade]] 0–1x Super Heavy Armor Piece ([[Super Heavy Armor]])
XP: 100-415 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 73-304
[Metadata]
ID: SUPER_TANK_ZOMBIE_#; MASTER_SUPER_TANK_ZOMBIE_#
The Super Tank Zombie is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that only spawns on [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] and above. They are immune to [[Knockback]] with very high [[Defense]] and deal heavy knockback but low [[Damage]]. It drops [[Super Heavy Armor]] which dungeoneers often use for [[Effective Health]].
Its stats, like any other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], scales based on which floor they spawn in. It can also have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified.
- Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]].
- Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]].
- Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire.
- Speedy makes the mob move much faster.
- Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing true damage.
## Spawning
Super Tank Zombies can spawn in rooms within [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] and above floors.
## Behavior
Super Tank Zombies slowly walk towards the player and attempt to hit them, dealing [[Damage]] and a large amount of [[Knockback]]. When hit, Super Tank Zombies take no [[Knockback]]. They have high [[Defense]].

## Trivia
- Players can take advantage of its slow speed, making it relatively easy to kill. However, if this spawns with the Speedy modifier, it's slowness is canceled out, making it much harder to kill.

# Tank Zombie
Aliases: Crypt Tank Zombie, Master Crypt Tank Zombie
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Tank Zombie]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 23-192
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 10%
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Entrance]]-Floor III ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]])
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,200-160,000; [[Defense]]: 2,000-2,300; [[Damage]]: 201-22,500
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x Heavy Armor Piece ([[Heavy Armor]]) 0–1x [[Premium Flesh]]
XP: 34-288 Combat XP ([[Combat]])

[Metadata]
ID: CRYPT_TANK_ZOMBIE_#; MASTER_CRYPT_TANK_ZOMBIE_#
Tank Zombie is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor I]] - [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Floor III]] and [[The Catacombs - Entrance|Entrance]].
Its health, like any other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], scale on which floor they spawn in. It can also have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].
## Spawning
Tank Zombies can spawn in rooms within [[The Catacombs - Entrance]], [[The Catacombs - Floor I|Floor I]], [[The Catacombs - Floor II|Floor II]], and [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Floor III]].
## Behavior
Tank Zombies walk slowly towards nearby players and try to hit them. They are immune to [[Knockback]], and have high [[Damage]] reduction. They deal large amounts of [[Knockback]] to players.

# Terracotta
Aliases: Terracotta (disambiguation)
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Confirmations needed, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Terracotta]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 309-557
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 50% (Normal) 0% (Master)

[Drops]
XP: 463-835 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 339-612
[Metadata]
ID: SADAN_STATUE_1; MASTER_SADAN_STATUE_1
The Terracotta is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] during the [[Sadan]] fight.
## Spawning
Sixteen Terracottas will appear during the first phase of the [[Sadan]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
## Behavior
Terracottas attack the player with the melee and ranged attacks of the [[Flower of Truth]]. The ranged attack can pass through walls. During the first phase of the [[Sadan]] fight, a "Sadan's Interest Meter" counts down from 2 minutes. While this counts down, killing a Terracotta will turn it into a skull on the ground, subtract an extra second from the timer, and respawn it 10 seconds later where the skull was.

# The Diamond Giant
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[The Diamond Giant]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 543
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 25,000,000; [[Damage]]: 17,600
[Drops]
XP: +4,072 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1629
[Metadata]
ID: SADAN_GIANT_1
The Diamond Giant is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that appears in the [[Sadan]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]. In [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], it is replaced with the [[Mutant Giant]].
## Spawning
The Diamond Giant appears in the [[Sadan]] fight arena in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]. It drops down to attack the player after [[Sadan|Sadan's Interest Level]] has been emptied for the second phase of the fight. In [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], all four giants are replaced with [[Mutant Giant|Mutant Giants]].
## Behavior
The Diamond Giant will run towards the player, damaging them when they get within range. They also occasionally throw a giant sword down onto the player, dealing 25,160 [[Damage]].

# The Diamond Giant (Watcher)

The Diamond Giant, titled Giant in game, is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that can be summoned by [[The Watcher]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]. They also [[The Diamond Giant|appear]] in the [[Sadan]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
## Spawning
The Diamond Giant has a 25% (1 in 4 spawns) chance to be spawned from [[The Watcher]] in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
## Behavior
The Diamond Giant will run towards the player, damaging them when they get within range. They also occasionally throw a giant sword down onto the player, dealing 25,160 [[Damage]] in regular mode or 200,000 [[Damage]] in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].

# The Watcher
Aliases: Blood Room, Lathrop, Seeing-Stone, The Watcher's Undeads, Watcher, Watchful Eye
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Article stubs, Catacombs, Dungeons, Minibosses, Bosses
[The Watcher]
Type: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Description: Stalker; This strange creature is roaming The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]]) to add powerful adventurers to its collection.
Mob Type: [[Arcane]]
[Metadata]
ID: THE_WATCHER_1; MASTER_THE_WATCHER_1
The Watcher is the [[Bosses|BOSS]] in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. In the [[The Catacombs - Entrance|Entrance]] floor, defeating them completes the run, but in all other floors, defeating them opens a portal to the main [[Bosses|BOSS]] of that floor.
## The Fight
### Overview
When the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] is first opened, The Watcher will say some dialogue, and then start spawning various Mobs. The amount spawned increases with higher floors, starting at 9 in [[The Catacombs - Entrance|Entrance]] and increasing by 1 with each floor. These include multiple [[Undead (Watcher)|Undeads]], as well as various [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] mobs in higher floors. These mobs first appear as heads in display cases on the walls, which the Watcher flies to when picking to spawn.
The Watcher cannot be damaged, and trying to do so will cause him to attack back with a laser. In [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Floor IV]] and above, he also has Watchful Eyes to prevent players from going more than several blocks up in the arena.
Players who are dead when the fight starts or die during the fight are reanimated as a hostile [[Undead (Watcher)|Wandering Soul]] that resembles the player who died. Killing a Wandering Soul will revive the player it resembles.
Once all of Mobs are summoned and killed, in the [[The Catacombs - Entrance|Entrance]] floor, the run completes. In all other floors, a portal will open to the main [[Bosses|BOSS]] of that floor, and 1-2 [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessings of Time V]] and a [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing of Power V]] will spawn. There is also a 1.23% (1 in 81.3 rolls) chance for an [[Optical Lens]] to drop.
### Arena
- The Catacombs - Floor I. [[The Catacombs - Floor I]].
- The Catacombs - Entrance. [[The Catacombs - Entrance]].
The Watcher's arena is fairly simple. There are 4 chains in the room with the Undead's and Player's heads on the wall. There are some corridors behind the Undead wall. A way to cheese the boss up to [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Floor III]] is to go on top of the portal, entrance door, or go on the second layer of the Undead. Keep in mind the top of the exit portal changes on every floor to represent the boss of that floor.
### Floor Differences
[Watchful Eye]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Special Behavior: Damages players who attempt to stay away from the fight off and stay on platforms
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]]
[Metadata]
ID: WATCHER_GUARDIAN_1; MASTER_WATCHER_GUARDIAN_1
Higher [[Catacombs|Floors]] have changes to the Watcher's behavior:
- Starting from [[The Catacombs - Floor II|Floor II]], the Undead gain special perks according to their type.
- Starting from [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Floor III]], [[Bonzo (Watcher)|Bonzo]] can appear reanimated at the start of the fight. The reanimated Bonzo can perform his fireworks ability when brought down below 50% [[Health]].
- Starting from [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Floor IV]], the Watcher will be next to 2 Watchful Eyes, which deals 25% of the player's [[Health]] as [[True Damage]] every second the player is too high up in the arena. Additionally, [[Scarf (Watcher)|Scarf]] can appear at the start instead of [[Bonzo (Watcher)|Bonzo]].
- Starting from [[The Catacombs - Floor V|Floor V]], all mobs spawned gain a random modifier. In just this floor, the modifier will be announced as dialogue before the fight begins. The [[Spirit Bear (Watcher)|Spirit Bear]] also can now spawn instead of [[Bonzo (Watcher)|Bonzo]] or [[Scarf (Watcher)|Scarf]].
|Modifier|Description|
|-|-|
|Healthy|Grants 1.3x [[Health]], a 5% (1 in 20 kills) chance to drop a [[Healing Tissue]], and a 0.4% (1 in 250) chance to drop a [[Vitamin Death]].|
|Stormy|Grants a 1% (1 in 100 kills) chance to drop Thunderlord VI ([[Thunderlord]]) on death. Every 5 seconds, there is also a 33.3% (1 in 3) chance to strike lightning to all players in a 6 block radius. This deals 2,000 [[Damage]] /2,500 [[Damage]] /5,000 [[Damage]] in F5-F7 respectively, and 45,000 [[Damage]] /60,000 [[Damage]] /70,000 [[Damage]] in M5-M7 respectively.|
|Golden|Grants full [[Golden Armor]] and drop 1 [[Gold Essence]] on death.|
|Speedy|Grants increased [[Speed]] and drops Dungeon Potions ([[Dungeon Potion]]) of the equivalent floor ([[Catacombs]]) tier.|
|Stealthy|Makes the mobs invisible, except for their name tags and armor.|
|Boomer|Makes the mobs wear TNT on their head, which is triggered when they die. After a few second it explodes in a 5x5 radius, dealing 100% of the player's [[Health]] as [[True Damage]]. This can be mitigated with [[Absorption]] and the [[Wither Cloak Sword]]. This also drops [[Superboom TNT]].|
- Starting from [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor VI]], The Watcher no longer announces the modifier of its summons before starting. Additionally, [[Livid (Watcher)|Livid]] can appear instead of [[Bonzo (Watcher)|Bonzo]], [[Scarf (Watcher)|Scarf]], or a [[Spirit Bear (Watcher)|Spirit Bear]], dropping a [[Livid Fragment]]. Additionally, [[Bonzo (Watcher)|Bonzo]] or [[Scarf (Watcher)|Scarf]] can now drop their own respective fragment items.
- In [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]], before any other spawns, one of [[Sadan]]'s giants ( [[Bigfoot (Watcher)|Bigfoot]], [[Jolly Pink Giant (Watcher)|Jolly Pink Giant]], [[L.A.S.R. (Watcher)|L.A.S.R.]], [[The Diamond Giant (Watcher)|The Diamond Giant]]) will be reanimated. The pillars will be removed in the arena, making space for the Giant, although there's a rare chance the pillars won't be removed. Each one has a chance to drop its respective [[Precursor Relic]], which can upgrade [[Wither Armor]] dropped from [[Necron]].
### Summoned Mobs
The Watcher will spawn multiple [[Undead (Watcher)|Undead]] mobs with various names and appearances. These include Mr. Dead, Revoker, Tear, Skull, Walker, Cannibal, Frost, Putrid, Reaper, Vader, Flamer, Freak, Leech, Mute, Ooze, Parasite, and Psycho. They can also appear as Wandering Souls.
Additionally, starting from [[The Catacombs - Floor III]], different reincarnations of previous [[Bosses|BOSS]] mobs will start spawning. In [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]], a random giant from the [[Sadan]] fight will also spawn.

|[[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]|50% (1 in 2 spawns)|Bonzo ([[Bonzo (Watcher)]]), Scarf ([[Scarf (Watcher)]])|

|[[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]|25% (1 in 4 spawns)|Bonzo ([[Bonzo (Watcher)]]), Scarf ([[Scarf (Watcher)]]), Spirit Bear ([[Spirit Bear (Watcher)]]), Livid ([[Livid (Watcher)]])|

## Lore
- Before becoming The Watcher, he was a human known as Lathrop, he was a pupil and adopted son of [[Dr. Emmett]] after his parents died during the [[Jythar|Calamity]]. Ever since [[The Breach]] on top of the mountain has opened, Lathrop started getting visions of a floating eye.
- One day he is asked by Dr. Emmett to deliver a letter to his colleague in [[Wilderness]]. He heads out and for some reason feels sad looking at the house he lived in. Entering the Inn, he was getting the feeling of Deja Vu. In [[Wilderness|the Inn]] he saw a man with black hair in black clothing. He recognized this from one of his visions. The mysterious man then leaves upstairs, a different man, a young assassin named [[Livid]], comes to Lathrop and questions him about the mysterious man, since he claims to have never seen in the Inn before. Lathrop answers Livid's question saying that the mysterious man is a merchant from far away land, not knowing what lead him to say that since he in fact didn't know him. Livid then leaves back to his table and Lathrop too goes upstairs.
- A while after, the mysterious man then left the Inn carrying Livid with him. Lathrop decides to follow him, as his feeling of Deja Vu grows stronger, despite him not remembering this. [[Necron]] figures out he is being followed and before the wooden entrance to [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] he asks his pursuer, why is he following him. Lathrop explains his situation with visions and Deja Vu, then explains that he saw that by following Necron to [[The Wither King|his master]], he will reach his full potential, as the door opened on their own, Necron urged Lathrop to follow him.
- In Catacombs he meets The Wither King who leads him to his laboratory, in front of the Seeing-Stone, which immediately triggers a vision in him, he sees a house with a woman (Kaeman's late wife, Sarah) and a child (Kaeman's deceased daughter, Mylah) inside, he recognizes the source of the command, Kaeman. The Wither King was impressed by Lathrop's natural abilities and in the following months, he made him use the Seeing-Stone again and again. Which transformed him to the limbless, one-eyed creature he now is.
- Some time after, he was tasked to see Kaeman's past and use the orb to create a world, identical to the one Kaeman once knew, in which he could once again be happy. He has succeeded. Kaeman thanked the Watcher, which was a rare display from him and told him to be loyal to his new master - Necron, while he decided to stay in an enclosed chamber, dreaming of a happier time.
- After the seclusion of their master, Necron decided to gather an army of necromancers for his goal and Watcher at some point wrote most of the [[Journal|Dungeon Journals]]. In [[Time Systems|1531 AE]], he watches the expedition to the Catacombs. He notices one particular member and his potential in necromancy, after learning his name, The Watcher decided to recruit [[Sadan]].
## Bugs
- Flamer can deal damage to everyone in this server instead of an area around it.
## Trivia
- In [[Journal|Journal - The Eye]], it is shown that The Watcher was related to [[Sadan]]'s interest in Necromancy.
- The Watcher's eye resembles the Demon Eye from Terraria. This is further supported by the fact that both have a chance to drop item called "Optical Lens" upon their defeat.
- The Watcher fight could be inspired by the first phase of the Brain of Cthulhu fight in Terraria, where it becomes invincible and it summons minions you must kill.
- The Watcher will revive a player when their Wandering Soul was killed. If Wandering Soul is killed after the respective player was revived in an another way, the killing of a Wandering Soul won't have any effect.
- The color of The Watcher's eye starts changing from a vivid green to a sickly yellow the deeper you go in the Catacombs, which slowly turns more orange when going into [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
- The Watcher is around a decade older than [[Necron]] according to [[Journal|Journal - The Follower]] and [[Journal|Journal - The Apprentice]], as Lathrop was born before the [[Jythar|Calamity]], while Necron was born shortly before the Wither King's attack.
- [[Necron]] has a connection to The Watcher, calling it 'The Eye' by mentioning it in his first welcome speech.
- It is implied that The Watcher cannot be killed.
- One unknown undead refers to The Watcher as "Stalker" in [[Journal|Journal - Aftermath]].
  - "Stalker" is also The Watcher's epitaph in [[Mort]]'s description of the Entrance floor.
- The Watcher's original name, Lathrop, is a reference to Dr. Emmett Lathrop Brown, more commonly known as Doc, from Back to the Future, as is his father figure, [[Dr. Emmett]].
- Previously, if players managed to enter the Blood Room without picking up the Blood Key, The Watcher would start spawning mobs anyway. As of May 11, 2026, known methods to achieve this without cheats have been patched, and if a player enters the Blood Room without the Blood Key being picked up first, the run is immediately failed with the following message: Due to violation of our server rules this instance has been terminated.

# Thorn
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed, Catacombs, Dungeons, Bosses, Boss Collections
[Thorn]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]
Special Behavior: Summons Spirit Animals
Description: Shaman Necromancer; Powerful Necromancer who specializes in animals. Calls himself a vegetarian, go figure.
Mob Type: Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]]) [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 4-6
[Metadata]
ID: THORN_1; MASTER_THORN_1
Thorn is the [[Bosses|BOSS]] of [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]. He is a ghast which spawns animals and can only be damaged by his Spirit Bow.
Thorn's remains re-appear on [[The Catacombs - Floor V|Floor V]], from which [[Livid]] drains energy in order to clone himself.

## Boss Fight
### Overview
Unlike most of the other bosses in The Catacombs, Thorn only has one apparent phase, same as Livid, however technically has different phases, but the differences are minuscule. Thorn will speed up the more he has been damaged and when he is about to die in one more hit, Thorn will start turning around, making it harder to hit him. Additionally, with each wave that comes, the Spirit Animals gain more HP, overall making it a longer fight.
After 15s of dialogue, Thorn will summon Spirit Animals which can deal damage to the player along with a new [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] called the Spirit Bear. A counter (indicated by a ring of [[Sea Lantern|Sea Lanterns]] and [[Block of Coal|Coal Blocks]] near the audience) shows how many Spirit Animals have been killed. When the ring fully lights up with Sea Lanterns, the Spirit Bear will spawn.
There is a crowd of enemies cheering for Thorn in the arena. While they can't attack the player, they can give helpful hints for Thorn's attacks. Although, if the player enters the crowds viewing area, swarms of spirit bats will attack the player and deal large quantities of damage.
Occasionally, one of the circles in the arena will change color, either green or orange. If it turns green, it will ready up a Healing Circle much like the Healer's one, but if it turns orange, it will start a fire on that circle. Thorn's spirit animals will take 5% of their HP every second they are in the fire.
### Spirit Bear
The Spirit Bear is a [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] summoned during the fight. It will be summoned once 25 Spirit Animals (or 30 in Master Mode) have been killed. It has 5M [[Health]] (or 180M [[Health]] in Master Mode) and can attack players with a Golden Sword or a Spirit Bow. The spirit bear will heal overtime, but very slowly.
When the Spirit Bear is killed, it drops a Spirit Bow. The player who picks it up only gets one chance to shoot Thorn. Each successful hit stuns Thorn for 5s, followed by a 1s cooldown before Thorn can be damaged again. Every hit from the Spirit Bow removes 1 [[Health]] from Thorn’s health. Once Thorn’s health reaches zero, they die.
### Summoned Mobs
Throughout the fight, various Spirit Mobs will spawn in.
- [[Spirit Bull]] - These rush towards the player to attack them. They also have a charge attack, dealing 30% of the base [[Damage]] in regular mode (846.6 ) and 75% of the base [[Damage]] in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] (53,550 ).
- [[Spirit Wolf]] - These will swarm players and attack them in large quantities. They deal 3.5x damage to players using the [[Tank]] [[Classes|Class]].
- [[Spirit Bat]] - These will swarm players who are in the air. They do not respect player invincibility frames. This means if a player jumps in the air and is swarmed by bats, they will be mowed to death in seconds. Can be deterred by decoys as they have no vanilla attacking ability and cannot destroy it.
- [[Spirit Rabbit]] - These will attack the player, launching them in the air, and then run away.
- [[Spirit Chicken]] - These slowly descends to the ground, strike nearby players with lightning. If it touches the ground, it will turn into a Chicken Mine. Players who walk over it take damage and are knocked into the air. The lightning attacks deal 1.5x the base [[Damage]] (1,392 ) in regular mode, and 0.35x the base [[Damage]] (9,408 ) in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
- [[Spirit Sheep]] - These run toward the player and explodes like a creeper. This deals up to 0.2x of the base [[Damage]], or 3,808 in regular mode and 133,280 in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], with the amount depending on close the player was to the explosion.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

[BOSS] Thorn: Today you'll be our spectacle. [BOSS] Thorn: Dance! Dance with my Spirit animals! And may you perish in a delightful way!|
|Attempting to attack [[Thorn]] Thorn without using a [[Spirit Bow]]|[BOSS] Thorn: Tickle tickle, you are in a pickle! [BOSS] Thorn: I'm already dead adventurer! Earthly weapons won't affect me! [BOSS] Thorn: Don't waste your energy on me, humans - I am Thorn the Spirit! [BOSS] Thorn: I love your fighting spirit, but I am immune to your tricks! [BOSS] Thorn: Trying to damage me like that...desperate...|

|If all players have won|[BOSS] Thorn: This is it...where shall I go now? [BOSS] Thorn: All those memories will be lost in time, like tears in rain... [BOSS] Thorn: Congratulations humans, you may pass.|

## Trivia
- The [[Spirit Bow]] used in the Thorn fight has some extra lore attached to it.
- Thorn's remains are seen in during the [[Livid]] boss fight in the form of a purple orb (|This Orb you see, is Thorn, or what is left of him.").
- The Spirit Bow specifically does approximately 75,000 [[Damage]] per hit against Thorn.
- One of Thorn's lines is "Round and round, another wound." which might have been intended to rhyme, even though it does not.
- The dialogue of Thorn's crowd contains many references.
  - "Another 2020 catastrophe." references the COVID-19 pandemic.
  - "Killing a spirit...what kind of man does that? ... No man at all." references Breaking Bad, in which Gustavo Fring asks Hector Salamanca "What kind of man talks to the DEA? No man, no man at all."
  - "Avenge Bonzo! #RememberBonzo" and "Remember Bonzo! #NeverForget" references [[Bonzo]], the boss of [[The Catacombs - Floor I]], hinting that the two are connected. Notably, Bonzo is a circus clown and Thorn hosts a performance.
  - "My disappointment is immeasurable and my day is ruined." references the quote of the same name, in which host of TheReportOfTheWeek, Reviewbrah, says the exact quote after tasting Popeyes' cheddar biscuit butterfly shrimp.
  - "Fs in the chat!" is a reference to the meme originating from 2014's Call of Duty Advanced Wafare, where there is an event in the campaign where you have to "Press F to pay respects", which eventually has been used by gamers in the event of a huge loss.
## Tips
- There is currently a bug that allows players can stun Thorn in place using a [[Tribal Spear]] or [[Bonemerang]]. This is commonly used by players to make the bossfight easier.
  - Attempting to stun Thorn but failing the stun whilst Thorn is still alive will make Thorn will become extremely fast. This is because when Thorn gets hit with the [[Tribal Spear]] or [[Bonemerang]] it treats it like he got hit with the [[Spirit Bow]] which increases his speed.
  - The [[Terminator]]'s "Salvation" ability can also cause him to reach this state but it is much more difficult, since the ability can only be cast after landing 3 hits with the bow.
