# Undead (Watcher)
Aliases: Cannibal, Flamer, Freak, Frost, Leech, Mr. Dead, Mute, Ooze, Parasite, Psycho, Putrid, Reaper, Revoker, Skull, Tear, Vader, Wandering Soul, Watcher Undead
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Confirmations needed, Mobs with Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Undead (Watcher)]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 70-374
Spawn Condition: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 14,000-6,000,000 (Base); [[Defense]]: 550-2,000; [[Damage]]: 1,440-97,200
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Beating Heart]]
XP: 105-561 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 77-411
[Metadata]
ID: WATCHER_SUMMON_UNDEAD_#; MASTER_WATCHER_SUMMON_UNDEAD_#
Undeads are a series of [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]] that can be summoned by [[The Watcher]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. They take on different appearances and names, including Mr. Dead, Revoker, Tear, Skull, Walker, Cannibal, Frost, Putrid, Reaper, Vader, Flamer, Freak, Leech, Mute, Ooze, Parasite, and Psycho. They can also appear as Wandering Souls.
## Spawning
Undeads are repeatedly spawned from [[The Watcher]] in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] and above. The amount spawned increases with higher floors, starting at 9 in [[The Catacombs - Entrance|Entrance]] and increasing by 1 with each floor.
## Behavior
Undeads will run towards nearby players to attack them with melee. In [[The Catacombs - Floor II]], some of them also have special behavior.
## Variants
Throughout [[The Watcher]]'s fight, multiple random Undeads will be spawned. Each version has the same base [[Stats]] and loot, but has a different name and skin. Multiple of the same type can exist. Each undead can also spawn with random armor pieces.
In [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] and above, special behaviors are added, as listed below. In [[The Catacombs - Floor V]] and above, they can have additional modifiers, as listed further down.
|Name|Abilities in Floor II+|
|-|-|
|Mr. Dead|Gains Strength for every Undead near it.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|
|Revoker|Can charge towards the player.[Confirm]|
|Tear|Has a Bleeding effect much like the [[Minotaur]].[Confirm]|
|Skull|Can throw a bone like Crypt Lurkers every 12 seconds.[Confirm]|
|Walker|Faster than other Undeads.[Confirm]|
|Cannibal|Targets Berserkers ([[Berserk]])|
|Frost|Targets Mages ([[Mage]])|
|Putrid|Targets Healers ([[Healer]])|
|Reaper|Targets Archers ([[Archer]])|
|Vader|Targets Tanks ([[Tank]])|
|Flamer|Explodes when killed, dealing 1% of nearby players' max [[Health]].|
|Freak|Deals 1.2x [[Damage]].|
|Leech|Heals for 2% of its max [[Health]] every second.|
|Mute|Prevents the player from hearing certain in-game sounds for 5 seconds.|
|Ooze|Has 1.25x [[Health]].|
|Parasite|Spawns 3 Parasites ([[Parasite (Silverfish)]]) when killed.|
|Psycho|Has a 1/3 chance of teleporting behind the player when attacked.|
|Wandering Soul|Wandering Souls take the appearance of players who die in a Dungeon run. When a Wandering Soul is killed, the corresponding player is respawned. Wandering Souls do not spawn if a [[Healer]] is active.|

### Modifiers
In [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] and above, the Ooze undead has 1.25x [[Health]], and the Freak undead has 1.2x [[Damage]].
Then, in [[The Catacombs - Floor V]] and above, there is a chance for each undead summoned by [[The Watcher]] to have one of the following modifiers. When one is chosen, all undeads summoned spawn with it.
|Modifier|Description|
|-|-|
|Healthy|Grants 1.3x [[Health]], a 5% (1 in 20 kills) chance to drop a [[Healing Tissue]], and a 0.4% (1 in 250) chance to drop a [[Vitamin Death]].|
|Stormy|Grants a 1% (1 in 100 kills) chance to drop Thunderlord VI ([[Thunderlord]]) on death. Every 5 seconds, there is also a 33.3% (1 in 3) chance to strike lightning to all players in a 6 block radius. This deals 2,000 [[Damage]] /2,500 [[Damage]] /5,000 [[Damage]] in F5-F7 respectively, and 45,000 [[Damage]] /60,000 [[Damage]] /70,000 [[Damage]] in M5-M7 respectively.|
|Golden|Grants full [[Golden Armor]] and drop 1 [[Gold Essence]] on death.|
|Speedy|Grants increased [[Speed]] and drops Dungeon Potions ([[Dungeon Potion]]) of the equivalent floor ([[Catacombs]]) tier.|
|Stealthy|Makes the mobs invisible, except for their name tags and armor.|
|Boomer|Makes the mobs wear TNT on their head, which is triggered when they die. After a few second it explodes in a 5x5 radius, dealing 100% of the player's [[Health]] as [[True Damage]]. This can be mitigated with [[Absorption]] and the [[Wither Cloak Sword]]. This also drops [[Superboom TNT]].|
At the end, on all floors, each Undead has their [[Health]] randomized by an amount between -2,000 and 6,000 [[Health]] based on the following formula:
Max Health=Health+(1000×rand⁡(−2,6))

# Undead Archer
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Undead Archer]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 159-450
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor II]]
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 400,000-60,000,000; [[Damage]]: 2,752-51,600
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Beating Heart]]
XP: 238-675 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 174-495
[Metadata]
ID: SCARF_ARCHER_1; MASTER_SCARF_ARCHER_1
The Undead Archer is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] during the [[Scarf]] fight.
## Spawning
An Undead Archer will appear twice during the [[Scarf]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]], once at the start of the fight and again after all the undead Archers have been slain.
## Behavior
The Undead Archer will, by default, shoot an arrow towards the player every few seconds. It also has several other attacks:
- Arrow Barrage - Shoots a large number of arrows in all directions.
- Teleport - Teleport himself away from danger, or a player into danger (Usually Warrior's charge).
- Help - Calls for help, causing the [[Undead Warrior]] to perform a Warrior's Charge to the player who dealt the most damage to the archer.

# Undead Mage
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Undead Mage]

[Metadata]
ID: SCARF_MAGE_1; MASTER_SCARF_MAGE_1
The Undead Mage is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] during the [[Scarf]] fight.
## Spawning
An Undead Mage will appear twice during the [[Scarf]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]], once at the start of the fight and again after all the undead warriors have been slain.
## Behavior
The Undead Mage will frequently run towards the player to attack them. They also have the following abilities:
- Wither Skulls - Shoots wither skulls towards the player while standing still, dealing 0.45x the base [[Damage]] (1,094.4 ) in regular mode and 0.65x (29,640 ) it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
- Magma Pit - Deals 0.15x the base [[Damage]] (364.8 ) in regular mode and 0.6x (27,360 ) it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
- Tsunami - Creates a wave that deals 0.25x the base [[Damage]] (608 ) in regular mode and 0.5x the base [[Damage]] (22,800 ) in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
- Help - Calls for help, causing the [[Undead Warrior]] to perform a Warrior's Charge to the player who dealt the most damage to the mage.

The Undead Priest is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] during the [[Scarf]] fight.
## Spawning
An Undead Priest will appear twice during the [[Scarf]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]], once at the start of the fight and again after all the undead warriors have been slain. In [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], two appear at once instead, for a total of four.
## Behavior
The Undead Priest will run around and occasionally use the following two abilities:
- Heal - Heals up itself and nearby Undeads.
- Help - Calls for help, causing the [[Undead Warrior]] to perform a Warrior's Charge to the player who dealt the most damage to the priest.

The Undead Skeleton is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Spawning
Undead Skeletons spawn when 3 Skeleton Skulls, which are spawned when a Skeleton-type Mob is killed, are close to each other. They will group together and form into an Undead Skeleton. This can happen on any floor in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Behavior
Undead Skeletons will try to shoot the player with a Bow, dealing [[Damage Calculation|Projectile Damage]].

## Spawning
An Undead Warrior will appear twice during the [[Scarf]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]], once at the start of the fight and again after all the undead warriors have been slain.

The Undead Warrior will run towards the player to attack them. They will also occasionally charge at the player, dealing 0.3x the base [[Damage]] (864 ) in regular mode, or 1x the base [[Damage]] (54,000 ) in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
